## Evidence Update Protocol

Only evidence/state cells change during implementation; names, classifications, origin/destination pairs, and configuration obligations stay frozen unless a fresh four-seat review amends the approved map. Before each behavior slice: record intended rows, observe the named RED for the intended reason, implement the minimum change, run focused Debug and Release GREEN, run the gate compile/full-suite checks, then append command and commit evidence. A required or unledgered skip is a release stop.

### Root Cause and Ordered RED/GREEN Trace

R126 encoded a package with a default random `packageInstanceID` and then scanned the complete JSON wire for the mutable stale-version token `404`. A valid UUID could itself contain `404`, so the broad replay oracle intermittently failed even when all structured mutable-format assertions passed. The test-only repair binds a deterministic package instance ID and proves that the same value is serialized both at the top level and in the contract context; it does not weaken or remove the full forbidden-value loop.

### Scope and Limitation

This evidence proves that R126's current fixture cannot collide with its own forbidden `404` token through a random package-instance nonce. It does not prove the broad whole-wire substring oracle universally collision-free: a future deterministic manifest, digest, or other payload value could still contain `404`. Such a collision remains a review stop rather than permission to weaken the oracle. R126 keeps its exact name, Gate 3 assignment, Required/Required obligations, and `None (true retirement)` classification; actual retirement evidence remains pending at Gate 3.

This is the test-ID manifest hash; it is distinct from the earlier `3a3840ba3276698ce77a507284ef36604c1f2347391ce2419813182948e28fcb` combined class-count/log-digest manifest. The post-commit record supersedes the pre-commit historical snapshot in the Gate Evidence Log without rewriting that snapshot.

The Gate 3 four-seat jury corrected the atomic implementation boundary to 19 total paths and returned `FINAL_GATE3_19_PATH_IMPLEMENTATION_GO`. The correction defers the `WorkbenchOrdering` change to the production-normalization stage because it still has two ordinary direct-user actor-aware callsites whose production normalization must remain atomic with the source change. The authorized RED stage is therefore limited to the nine pre-production paths named by the jury: `Package.swift`, the five existing MindDeskCore test sources, the two literal fixture resources, and this ledger. No `Sources` path is authorized in the RED stage.

This RED stage retires exactly R027–R037, R056–R059, R069–R098, and R099–R129 (76 tests) and adds exactly A035–A037 (3 tests). Its configuration-specific inventory equation is therefore `657 - 76 + 3 = 584` in both Debug and Release. M003–M040 were already completed as count-neutral Gate 2B migrations; Gate 3 may narrow only the specifically authorized existing destination bodies and must not repeat, rename, re-count, or remigrate any M003–M040 row. The expected 12-class partition after the RED edit is `335 + 19 + 1 + 16 + 29 + 134 + 9 + 11 + 3 + 10 + 6 + 11 = 584`.

### Gate 3 Pre-Production RED Evidence

The compile-safe RED slice uses two hand-authored literal resources processed only by `MindDeskCoreTests`; neither fixture is generated from a production builder. Debug `swift test list` and Release `swift test -c release list` both completed successfully after the test-only resource and source edits and produced identical manifests containing 584 unique IDs with zero duplicates. The observed class partition is `CoreBehaviorTests 335`, `ProposalReviewTests 19`, `AgentIntegrationContractTests 1`, `ValidationReportTests 16`, `LegacyReviewWireCompatibilityTests 29`, `AppBehaviorTests 134`, `ManifestImportServiceTests 9`, `PersistentStoreBootstrapIntegrationTests 11`, `CanvasReviewCapabilityLockTests 3`, `MindDeskJSONDocumentClassifierTests 10`, `LegacyReviewImportRejectionTests 6`, and `S0SurfaceAbsenceTests 11`; it sums to 584 and proves `657 - 76 + 3 = 584`. All 76 exact retirement origins in R027–R037, R056–R059, R069–R098, and R099–R129 are absent. All 38 M003–M040 origins are absent and every destination is present exactly once. Each retirement row now records pre-production retirement verification while leaving production GREEN pending.

After fourth-seat `FINAL_GATE3_RED_REPAIR_GO`, the two Foundation container-equality calls were replaced by one recursive strict JSON comparator. It requires exact dictionary key sets, array count/order, null and string identity, uses CoreFoundation type IDs to prevent Boolean/number bridging, and requires equal non-Boolean Objective-C numeric types and values. A036 contains explicit `true != 1` and `false != 0` oracle canaries. M006 additionally proves fixture decode→encode→redecode typed equality and recursive strict whole-JSON equality; its exact Debug run passed 1/0/0, so no fixture drift or fixture accommodation was introduced.

Debug `swift test --filter LegacyReviewWireCompatibilityTests` and Release `swift test -c release --filter LegacyReviewWireCompatibilityTests` each built successfully, exited 1, and executed 29 tests with 16 expected assertion failures and zero unexpected failures. In each configuration the same three new cases are RED for the intended old-production reasons: A035 has one source-absence failure because `MindDeskProposalReviewPolicy` remains in `MindDeskProposalEnvelope.swift`; A036 has eight failures because decoding reconstructs current summary, guide, contract, catalog, help, report/policy, and whole-package JSON instead of preserving the deliberately noncurrent stored fixture; A037 has seven failures because missing required contract/operation/capability fields still decode through fallbacks. The other 26 Legacy-wire cases are GREEN, including the narrowed M003–M040 destinations. No unresolved symbol, resource-loading failure, fixture parse failure, or compilation failure is counted as behavior RED.

The two retained Validation non-replay tests were verified separately rather than attributed to the Legacy filter. Debug and Release `--filter ValidationReportTests.testValidationReportDecodeReencode` each executed exactly 2/0/0: `testValidationReportDecodeReencodeDoesNotLaunderRawManifestIssuePayloads` and `testValidationReportDecodeReencodeRedactsInstructionOverridePhrasesWithoutSecrets`.

Full Debug `swift test` and full Release `swift test -c release` each exited 1 and executed exactly 584 tests with 16 failures, zero unexpected failures, and no skipped-test case or skip aggregate. In both configurations the only failed cases were A035, A036, and A037 with the same 1/8/7 intended assertion split; all remaining 581 cases passed. A static six-body audit found each authorized retained body exactly once: the Core body contains ten direct-user actor-aware assertions and no Agent actor assertion; the three Proposal bodies contain no `MindDeskProposalValidationReport` reference and retain ordered native diagnostic paths; and the two Validation bodies contain no current/default report-policy builder dependency.

Immediately before this evidence append, `git diff --check` exited 0. The final post-append rerun also exits 0. `git status --short --untracked-files=all` shows only the authorized nine pre-production paths: six existing test/package paths, this ledger, and the two new literal fixture files; `git diff --cached --name-only` and `git diff --name-only -- Sources` are both empty. No path is staged or committed.

## Evidence and Limitations

- Gate 0 proves only the pre-implementation Debug/Release baseline and the exact naming arithmetic. It does not prove future S0 behavior, release policy, signing, notarization, performance, or final count.
- Gate 0, Gate 1A, and Gate 1B complete ephemeral logs were hashed and summarized before cleanup but are not checked-in artifacts; the command transcript plus this ledger records their digests.
- No production source, test source, package/workflow configuration, experiment, run, artifact registry, or ResearchVault content changed during Gate 0.
- Gate 1A changed only its five approved code/test paths plus this ledger and was committed as `72a31de00081e87a5d938e33479b705597b966f0`. It proves the permanent lock and bounded classifier contract.
- Gate 1B changed only its five approved code/test paths plus this ledger and was committed as `cf0f88f`. It proves bounded URL/Data manifest admission, neutral permanent-lock rejection, the UI rollback boundary, and the recorded migration rows; it does not remove legacy review runtime or product surfaces assigned to Gate 2.
- Gate 2A evidence verifies A019–A023, focused Debug/Release behavior, fresh isolated builds, the original independent 799/0/0 full suites, unique-name arithmetic, the bounded single Release disposition 799/0/0, and the recorded source audit. The later pre-commit R126 anomaly was recorded and bounded without retry; the fresh restoration review returned `C0 / M0 / m0 — FINAL_RESTORE_GO`, and the exact nine approved paths were committed as `762a81dfb924626cefa9fb7d456f71647d1fec6e`.
- Gate 2A early RED/GREEN command times and complete console-log hashes were not persistently captured. The fourth-seat focused scratches were recoverably moved to Trash, but no persistent console logs were produced, so their output SHA-256 values were not captured. No missing timestamp or hash is backfilled.
- Nine final build/full-suite scratch, log, and list artifacts plus two focused-jury scratch directories—eleven Gate 2A artifacts total—were recoverably moved to `/Users/joshua/.Trash`; the four complete final logs and canonical test list were hashed before the move.
- The fourth-seat AppBehavior Finder filters matched zero tests and emitted warnings in both configurations; they are not PASS evidence. Only the correct CoreBehavior Finder filters, each 1/0/0, are recorded as focused evidence.
- The device/inode token detects path-object replacement between metadata lookup and open. It intentionally does not claim to detect same-inode in-place content mutation; bytes are still read from one verified descriptor and pass the Data-size/classifier/decoder chain.
- M044's dynamic evidence uses the sole importer-boundary invocation plus context-scoped `willSave`/`didSave` and SwiftData state arrays. SwiftData does not expose a direct subclassable `insert` hook; A014/A018 prove rejection returns before the sole importer boundary rather than adding a production-only test seam.
- The initial missing-symbol compile RED was observed and transcript-bound, but its first invocation did not capture a wall-clock timestamp; the later placeholder and all classifier RED/GREEN/final commands are timestamped above. No timestamp was invented.
- The later R126 exact Release rerun passed 1/0/0 but has no retained complete log or output hash. The UUID sample and loop reproduction supporting the broad-oracle root cause were transcript-observed only; their numeric observations are not promoted to durable benchmark evidence.
- The bounded Release disposition run is durable command evidence for Gate 2A commit eligibility only. Its complete log and scratch remain recoverable in `/Users/joshua/.Trash`; the separate R126 slice above, rather than that bounded run, establishes deterministic current-fixture evidence.
- R126's separate stabilization changes only its test fixture and mirror assertions. Its RED log, focused/full logs, 200 individual repeat logs, metadata, and ordered manifests are retained recoverably in Trash. The manifest data rows preserve the XCTest summary's leading tab as an explicit empty field; the audit matched this representation and every individual log digest.
- R126's fixed package-instance fixture removes the observed random-nonce collision, but the whole-wire substring oracle is not claimed universally collision-free. A future deterministic manifest, digest, or payload collision remains a review stop, and R126 still requires true retirement at Gate 3.
- Gate 2B pre-production evidence proves the exact old-tree absence witnesses and ordinary-surface characterization. The initial compile-only invocation exposed two missing `try` markers in new test-helper calls and is not represented as product RED. The first execution-evidence review then found and stopped on a false-positive A033 type-signature regex; that interim evidence is explicitly superseded, and the repaired-oracle evidence is separately hash-bound.
